[TITLE] Article: LLM Reliability Layers Under Test: Accuracy, Yield, and the Limits of Verification
[LINK] https://www.infoq.com/articles/llm-reliability-layers-limits-accuracy/?utm_campaign=infoq_content&utm_source=infoq&utm_medium=feed&utm_term=AI%2C+ML+%26+Data+Engineering
[PUBLISHED] 2026-11-10 18:00 KST
[SOURCE] InfoQ
[TOPIC] 모델
[SUMMARY]
Verification layers sort outputs by trust rather than fixing errors. In a benchmark of six reliability layers, voting and self-correction failed to recover accuracy - they only withheld doubtful records. Production systems gain more reliability by replacing generation with deterministic lookups, tracking yield over raw accuracy, and resolving extraction bottlenecks before stacking checks. By Pushpdeep Mishra, Wejdan Bagais

[PUBLISHED] 2026-11-10 23:07 KST
[SOURCE] The Decoder
[TOPIC] 산업
[SUMMARY]
Anthropic's Fable 5.2 completes 41.3 percent of real freelance jobs at professional quality on the Remote Labor Index, more than double the next-best systems Opus 5.5 and GPT 6 Astra. A year ago the top score was 2.5 percent. Areas like product design and animation remain a weak spot.
